# BroadLink OTA firmware

OTA firmware for BroadLink and OEM devices. Original filenames and source URLs are preserved; identical files are stored once, with source-based subfolders for filename collisions. Archive downloads include the member name.

The table identifies intended products, platforms, decimal device-type IDs and versions. Unconfirmed chipsets and unknown versions are marked explicitly. Device names use vendor metadata and the [python-broadlink supported-device table](https://github.com/mjg59/python-broadlink/blob/master/broadlink/__init__.py). Confirm hardware compatibility before flashing; shared offerings do not establish compatibility, and source URLs may no longer work.

| File | Intended device / product | Platform | Device type | Version / build | Original download URL |
